CONTEXT
- Project: Task_Authoring, Python 3 stdlib-only tools that author and check HarmonyGames benchmark tasks (V3: six
  Opus 5.5 runs per task; four difficulty gates on a rubric x run matrix). The tools grade platform trajectories against
  a "gradable surface" (a proxy grader), attribute register errors to designed mechanisms (node_verdicts), keep a
  cross-task evidence ledger (Beta priors per mechanism family), and forecast the six-run gates (hardness_forecast).
- P1 is a set of fixes ("rows"), specified below (quoted from plans/Learnings_And_Improvements_2026-10-25.md §4.2) with
  the user's decisions. Hard rules: stdlib only; SQL is parsed as data, never executed; the frozen backtest copies
  (support/backtests/season_pass/rev13, rev14) are read-only; every self-test temp folder is under support/scratch/;
  self-tests must not write the shared caches (support/local_search, support/hardness_ledger): they run in a sandbox
  workspace (tools/selftest_sandbox.py); every new test plants the failure it guards against.
- The diffs are exact: each file against its post-move content (sha256 verified against the post-move fingerprint),
  i.e. the whole P1 change of that file. "(added in P1)" files are new.
- "Falsification" scripts undo each fix in memory (monkeypatching the old behaviour back) and show that the self-test
  planting it fails; a control run (nothing undone) passes.


ROW SPECS (V1, M5, M7, WT, E2E)
| V1 | P1 | Intake provenance, with one owner of model labels shared by `trajectory_ledger` and `difficulty_stats`' model gate: strip only bracketed suffixes and compare exactly; `<synthetic>` stays out of the model check but counts under completeness; an unknown label gives UNKNOWN. `mixed_model` and `v3_valid` are separate outcomes (2026-10-25 review). Failures are attributed to the deciding model (brief writer, writing thread) | default expected model: all five revision-14 runs v3-invalid; `--expected-model claude-opus-4-8`: pre, r1, r2 and r6 mixed, r3 unmixed; a synthetic Opus 5.5 run with `[1m]` and `<synthetic>` labels is v3-valid; an off-model subagent and a removed fallback event still flag |
| E2E | P1 (2026-10-25 review) | One command runs the five revision-14 runs through `trajectory_ledger` → `node_verdicts` → `surface_grader` → a temp `hardness_ledger` → `hardness_forecast`, inside the sandbox | the exact failed-slot sets of record; r3's S43/S44 (8-character SHA prefixes) and S69–S71 (`slack_conversations_add_message`) pass; the pre-check is v3-invalid and mixed, with 0 designed landings, and SP-110 is a control record for M2 from an Opus 5 brief (declared in a fixture wrapper if the frozen solver can't express it; never edit the snapshot) |
| M5 | P1 | `oracle_kit` determinism across processes (different `PYTHONHASHSEED`); golden values through `hg.py records` | self-test |
| M7 | P1 | Planted defects for the uncovered check families (audit M7) | checker cases |
| WT | P1 (2026-10-25) | Write boundary: `tests/test_v3_stage_gate.py` and `difficulty_stats`' self-test put their temp folders under the scratch root, even outside the sandbox | the shared-cache fingerprint is unchanged by both; neither is run before the fix |

USER DECISIONS (V1): the default expected model comes from the task context (baseline_model, else the V3 policy model
claude-opus-5-5). Labels: strip only a bracketed suffix, then compare exactly. <synthetic> is excluded from the model
check but counted. An unrecognised label is UNKNOWN, never valid. mixed_model and v3_valid are separate outcomes. One
owner (difficulty_stats) shared by trajectory_ledger and the six-run model gate. Expected outcomes: default expected
model -> all five rev14 runs v3-invalid; --expected-model claude-opus-4-8 -> pre, r1, r2, r6 mixed, r3 unmixed; a
synthetic Opus 5.5 run is v3-valid.

USER DECISION (WT): tests/test_v3_stage_gate.py and difficulty_stats' self-test put their temp folders under the
scratch root, even outside the sandbox; neither is run before the fix.

USER DECISION (E2E): one command runs the five rev14 runs through trajectory_ledger -> node_verdicts ->
surface_grader -> a temp hardness_ledger -> hardness_forecast inside the sandbox. Assert: the failed-slot sets of record;
r3's S43/S44 and S69-S71 pass; the pre-check is v3-invalid and mixed with 0 designed landings, and SP-110 is a control
record for M2 from an Opus 5 brief (a fixture wrapper declares it if the frozen solver can't; never edit the snapshot).

+# ------------------------------------------------------------------ model labels (the one owner: trajectory_ledger, the
+# model gate, stage_gate's release check and surface_grader all classify labels here; decision 2026-10-25)
+SYNTHETIC_MODEL_LABELS = ("<synthetic>",)          # harness messages, not a model: never checked, counted apart
+RX_MODEL_ID = re.compile(r"claude-[a-z]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)+")
+
+
+def model_id(label) -> str:
+    """The label with only a bracketed suffix stripped: claude-opus-5-5[1m] -> claude-opus-5-5. Nothing else changes
+    (V1: strip only a bracketed suffix, then compare exactly; whitespace is part of the label)."""
+    return re.sub(r"\[[^\[\]]*\]$", "", str(label or ""))
+
+
+def classify_model(label, expected: str | None = None) -> str:
+    """expected · other · synthetic · unknown. The comparison is exact after model_id(): claude-opus-5 is not
+    claude-opus-5-5, and neither is claude-opus-5-5-20261001. A label that is not a model id is unknown."""
+    raw = str(label or "")
+    if raw in SYNTHETIC_MODEL_LABELS:
+        return "synthetic"
+    mid = model_id(raw)
+    if not RX_MODEL_ID.fullmatch(mid):
+        return "unknown"
+    return "expected" if mid == model_id(expected or V3_POLICY["model"]) else "other"
+
+
 def is_opus_55(model: str) -> bool:
-    def normalized(value):
-        value = re.sub(r"^claude[-\s]+", "", str(value).strip().casefold())
-        return re.sub(r"[.\s]+", "-", value)
-    expected, actual = normalized(V3_POLICY["model"]), normalized(model)
-    return bool(re.fullmatch(re.escape(expected) + r"(?:[-@][a-z0-9.\-]+)?", actual))
+    return classify_model(model) == "expected"
+
+
+def check_models(labels, expected: str | None = None) -> dict:
+    """A run's model labels against the expected model: {verdict: VALID | INVALID | UNKNOWN | MISSING, models,
+    off_model, unknown, synthetic}. Synthetic labels never decide; an unknown label is never valid."""
+    expected = expected or V3_POLICY["model"]
+    cls = [(str(m), classify_model(m, expected)) for m in labels or []]
+    real = sorted({model_id(m) for m, c in cls if c in ("expected", "other")})
+    off = sorted({m for m, c in cls if c == "other"})
+    unknown = sorted({m for m, c in cls if c == "unknown"})
+    verdict = ("UNKNOWN" if unknown else "INVALID" if off else "VALID" if real else "MISSING")
+    return {"verdict": verdict, "expected": expected, "models": real, "off_model": off, "unknown": unknown,
+            "synthetic": sum(1 for _, c in cls if c == "synthetic")}
+
+
+def message_models(events: list) -> list[dict]:
+    """Every model label an export carries, with where it came from: the init event, and each assistant message
+    (main thread, or the subagent of a brief through parent_tool_use_id)."""
+    out = []
+    for i, event in enumerate(events if isinstance(events, list) else []):
+        if not isinstance(event, dict):
+            continue
+        # a label is collected whenever the field is present: an explicit "" or null is a label (UNKNOWN), which is
+        # different from a message that carries no model field at all (V1)
+        if event.get("type") == "system" and event.get("subtype") == "init" and "model" in event:
+            out.append({"event": i, "label": str(event["model"]), "source": "init", "parent": None})
+        message = event.get("message")
+        if event.get("type") == "assistant" and isinstance(message, dict) and "model" in message:
+            parent = event.get("parent_tool_use_id")
+            out.append({"event": i, "label": str(message["model"]), "source": "subagent" if parent else "main",
+                        "parent": parent})
+    return out
+
+
+def model_provenance(events: list, expected: str | None = None) -> dict:
+    """Run-level model provenance from every message label. mixed_model and validity are separate outcomes:
+      mixed_model     two or more distinct model ids among the messages (synthetic labels excluded)
+      valid           VALID / INVALID / UNKNOWN / MISSING against `expected` (default: the V3 policy model)
+      v3_valid        True / False / None (UNKNOWN or MISSING) against the V3 policy model
+    off_model lists each off-model label with its source (main thread or subagent), count, first and last event and
+    the fallback event before it, if any."""
+    expected = expected or V3_POLICY["model"]
+    msgs = message_models(events)
+    falls = [i for i, e in enumerate(events if isinstance(events, list) else []) if isinstance(e, dict)
+             and e.get("type") == "system" and e.get("subtype") == "model_refusal_fallback"]
+    labels = [m["label"] for m in msgs]
+    mine, v3 = check_models(labels, expected), check_models(labels, V3_POLICY["model"])
+    groups = {}
+    for m in msgs:
+        if classify_model(m["label"], expected) != "other":
+            continue
+        g = groups.setdefault((m["label"], m["source"]), {"label": m["label"], "id": model_id(m["label"]),
+                                                         "source": m["source"], "count": 0, "first_event": m["event"],
+                                                         "last_event": m["event"], "subagent_briefs": set()})
+        g["count"] += 1
+        g["last_event"] = m["event"]
+        if m["parent"]:
+            g["subagent_briefs"].add(m["parent"])
+    off = []
+    for g in groups.values():
+        before = [f for f in falls if f <= g["first_event"]]
+        off.append(dict(g, subagent_briefs=len(g["subagent_briefs"]), after_fallback_event=before[-1] if before else None))
+    v3_valid = {"VALID": True, "INVALID": False}.get(v3["verdict"])
+    if falls or len(mine["models"]) > 1:
+        v3_valid = False
+    return {"expected_model": expected, "models": mine["models"], "mixed_model": len(mine["models"]) > 1,
+            "valid": mine["verdict"], "v3_valid": v3_valid,
+            "v3_verdict": v3["verdict"], "off_model": sorted(off, key=lambda x: x["first_event"]),
+            "unknown_labels": mine["unknown"], "synthetic_messages": mine["synthetic"], "fallback_events": falls,
+            "rule": "labels compared exactly after stripping a bracketed suffix; <synthetic> is a harness message, "
+                    "counted apart; an unrecognised label is UNKNOWN, never valid"}

+def precheck_provenance(events: list) -> dict:
+    """The pre-check gate's provenance decision in one testable place: completed/current check_models is VALID,
+    model_provenance is v3-valid, not mixed, and has no fallback events."""
+    ev = events if isinstance(events, list) else []
+    prov = model_provenance(ev)
+    labels = [m["label"] for m in message_models(ev)]
+    ok = (check_models(labels)["verdict"] == "VALID" and prov["v3_valid"] is True
+          and not prov["mixed_model"] and not prov["fallback_events"])
+    return {"ok": ok, "provenance": prov, "models": labels}
+
+
+def trajectory_provenance(path: Path) -> dict:
+    """Run-level provenance from the same events used by the pre-check and baseline gates. A missing/blank label,
+    a fallback event or a mixed label set is never accepted as clean Opus 5.5 provenance."""
+    if not path.stat().st_size:
+        return {"models": [], "mixed_model": False, "v3_valid": None, "fallback_events": [],
+                "unknown_labels": [], "valid": "MISSING"}
+    events = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    return model_provenance(events if isinstance(events, list) else [])
+

+def _self_test_models() -> None:

+CHANNEL_KINDS = ("scale", "chain", "delegation", "synthesis", "tool_surface")

+def controls_of(mod, revision=None) -> dict:
+    """{rule: {"members": [...], "over_application": operator or None}} for the rules that declare control members."""
+    return {r: {"members": list(v.get("controls") or []), "over_application": v.get("over_application")}
+            for r, v in active_rules(mod, revision).items() if v.get("controls")}
+
+
+def channels_of(mod) -> dict:
+    """The declared complexity channels: {channel: {kind, degrade, members, ...}}."""
+    return {c: dict(v) for c, v in (getattr(mod, "CHANNELS", None) or {}).items()}
+
+
+def _moved(base: dict, alt: dict, keys, fields) -> list:
+    return sorted(k for k in keys if k in base and k in alt and any(base[k].get(f) != alt[k].get(f) for f in fields))
+

+def check_controls(mod, state, rows, out, revision=None, slot_ids=None) -> None:
+    """contract.controls (see the module docstring). Nothing declared: PASS."""
+    ctl, ch = controls_of(mod, revision), channels_of(mod)
+    if not ctl and not ch:
+        _check(out, "contract.controls", True, "no control members or complexity channels declared")
+        return
+    problems, notes = [], []
+    rules = active_rules(mod, revision)
+    for r, c in ctl.items():
+        missing = [k for k in c["members"] if k not in rows]
+        if missing:
+            problems.append(f"{r}: control members {missing[:6]} are not solver keys")
+        op = c["over_application"]
+        if not op:
+            notes.append(f"{r}: {len(c['members'])} control(s), no over-application operator (every trial HELD)")
+            continue
+        fields = list(rules[r].get("decides") or []) + [class_field(mod)]
+        try:
+            moved = _moved(rows, mod.solve(state, degrade={op}), c["members"], fields)
+        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - a refused operator is the finding
+            problems.append(f"{r}: over_application {op!r} refused by solve(): {type(error).__name__}: {error}")
+            continue
+        if moved:
+            # a control whose over-application value is exactly the rule's ablation is not separable from the rule's
+            # own error: it must not become a landing (H1 residual a)
+            abl = mod.solve(state, disable={r})
+            dg = mod.solve(state, degrade={op})
+            same = [k for k in moved if k in abl and k in dg
+                    and all(dg[k].get(f) == abl[k].get(f) for f in fields)]
+            if same:
+                problems.append(f"{r}: over_application {op!r} equals the rule ablation for control member(s) {same[:4]}"
+                                f": the control cannot be separated from a rule error")
+            else:
+                notes.append(f"{r}: {len(c['members'])} control(s); {op} moves {moved}")
+        else:
+            problems.append(f"{r}: over_application {op!r} moves no control member's {fields}")
+    fields = sorted({f for v in rows.values() for f in v if f != "evidence"})
+    for cid, c in ch.items():
+        members, op = list(c.get("members") or []), c.get("degrade")
+        if c.get("kind") not in CHANNEL_KINDS:
+            problems.append(f"channel {cid}: kind {c.get('kind')!r} is not one of {CHANNEL_KINDS}")
+            continue
+        if c.get("latent") not in (None, "member", "batch", "run"):
+            problems.append(f"channel {cid}: latent {c.get('latent')!r} is not member, batch or run")
+            continue
+        if not op and c.get("slots"):
+            unknown = [s for s in c["slots"] if slot_ids is not None and s not in slot_ids]
+            if unknown:
+                problems.append(f"channel {cid}: slots {unknown} are not surface slots")
+            else:
+                notes.append(f"channel {cid} ({c['kind']}): fails surface slots {c['slots']}"
+                             + ("" if slot_ids is not None else " (not checked: no surface)"))
+            continue
+        if not members or not op:
+            problems.append(f"channel {cid}: needs members and a degrade operator, or surface slots")
+            continue
+        try:
+            moved = _moved(rows, mod.solve(state, degrade={op}), members, fields)
+        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001
+            problems.append(f"channel {cid}: degrade {op!r} refused by solve(): {type(error).__name__}: {error}")
+            continue
+        if moved:
+            notes.append(f"channel {cid} ({c['kind']}): {op} moves {len(moved)} of {len(members)} member(s)")
+        elif c.get("slots"):
+            unknown = [s for s in c["slots"] if slot_ids is not None and s not in slot_ids]
+            if unknown:
+                problems.append(f"channel {cid}: slots {unknown} are not surface slots")
+            else:
+                notes.append(f"channel {cid} ({c['kind']}): register-invisible; judged on surface slots {c['slots']}")
+        else:
+            problems.append(f"channel {cid}: degrade {op!r} moves no member's value and it declares no surface slots")
+    _check(out, "contract.controls", not problems, "; ".join(problems or notes))
+

+_PROBE = ("import hashlib, sys\n"
+          "sys.path[:0] = [sys.argv[1]]\n"
+          "import oracle_kit as ok\n"
+          "rows = ok.truth(ok.load_solver(sys.argv[2]), sys.stdin.read(), sys.argv[3] or None)\n"
+          "print(hashlib.sha256(ok.canonical(rows).encode()).hexdigest())\n")
+HASH_SEEDS = ("1", "2")

+def cross_process(solver_path, sql_text: str, revision=None) -> dict:
+    """{PYTHONHASHSEED: sha256 of the canonical truth} from fresh processes (M5)."""
+    out = {}
+    for s in HASH_SEEDS:
+        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONHASHSEED=s, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
+        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "-c", _PROBE, str(HERE), str(solver_path), revision or ""], env=env,
+                           input=sql_text, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=1800)
+        out[s] = r.stdout.strip() if r.returncode == 0 else "ERROR " + (r.stderr.strip()[-160:] or f"exit {r.returncode}")
+    return out
+

+def check_deterministic(mod, sql_text, revision, out, solver_path=None) -> dict:
     a = mod.solve(mod.load_state(sql_text, revision))
     st = mod.load_state(sql_text, revision)
     b_keys = list(reversed(list(a)))
     b = mod.solve(st, keys_=b_keys)
     b = {k: b[k] for k in a} if set(b) == set(a) else b
-    ok = canonical(a) == canonical(b)
-    _check(out, "contract.deterministic", ok, f"two fresh solves (forward and reverse key order) give identical output "
-           f"({len(a)} keys, sha {hashlib.sha256(canonical(a).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]})" if ok else
-           "two fresh solves disagree: the solver depends on order, time or global state")
+    mine = hashlib.sha256(canonical(a).encode()).hexdigest()
+    same = canonical(a) == canonical(b)
+    procs = cross_process(solver_path, sql_text, revision) if solver_path else {}
+    across = all(h == mine for h in procs.values())
+    _check(out, "contract.deterministic", same and across,
+           (f"two fresh solves (forward and reverse key order) give identical output ({len(a)} keys, sha {mine[:12]})"
+            + (f", and so do fresh processes with PYTHONHASHSEED {' and '.join(procs)}" if procs else "")) if same and across
+           else "two fresh solves disagree: the solver depends on order, time or global state" if not same
+           else f"fresh processes disagree ({ {s: h[:12] for s, h in procs.items()} } vs {mine[:12]} in this process): "

+def base_records(routed: list, task_dir=None) -> tuple:
+    """({record id: complete record | None (absent on a complete scan) | "INCOMPLETE"}, error) for base records, read
+    through tools/hg.py records (the Evals record helper; its caches stay in the bundle). routed: [(id, route)].
+    A non-zero hg.py exit or a reply without checks is an error: a routed record that cannot be read must fail the
+    golden check, never pass as "unverified"."""
+    import selftest_sandbox
+    groups = {}
+    for rid, rt in routed:
+        groups.setdefault((rt["service"], rt["file"], rt["pointer"], rt["field"]), set()).add(rid)
+    order = sorted(groups)
+    req = {"checks": [{"id": f"g{i}", "service": s, "file": f, "pointer": p, "field": fld, "values": sorted(groups[k])}
+                      for i, (k, (s, f, p, fld)) in enumerate(zip(order, order))]}
+    with selftest_sandbox.scratch_tmp("oracle_kit_golden_") as tmp:
+        path = Path(tmp) / "record_checks.json"
+        path.write_text(json.dumps(req))
+        cmd = [sys.executable, "-B", str(HERE / "hg.py")] + (["--task-dir", str(task_dir)] if task_dir else []) + \
+            ["records", "--request", str(path)]
+        r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=1800)
+    try:
+        doc = json.loads(r.stdout)
+    except ValueError:
+        return {}, f"tools/hg.py records exit {r.returncode}: {(r.stderr or r.stdout).strip()[-160:]}"
+    if r.returncode != 0:
+        return {}, f"tools/hg.py records exit {r.returncode}: {(r.stderr or r.stdout).strip()[-160:]}"
+    if not (doc.get("checks") or {}):
+        return {}, f"tools/hg.py records returned no checks (exit {r.returncode})"
+    got = {}
+    for i, k in enumerate(order):
+        c = (doc.get("checks") or {}).get(f"g{i}") or {}
+        found = {m.get("value"): m.get("record") for m in c.get("matches") or []}
+        complete = c.get("scan_complete") is True
+        for rid in groups[k]:
+            got[rid] = (found.get(rid) if complete else "INCOMPLETE")
+    return got, None
+

 def load(path) -> dict:
+    """A GRADABLE_SURFACE file, or a revision-14 legacy surface converted in memory (from_revision14)."""
     s = json.loads(Path(path).read_text())
+    if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("kind") == REV14_KIND:

     if not isinstance(s, dict) or s.get("kind") != "GRADABLE_SURFACE":

+def from_revision14(s: dict, deliverables: dict | None = None, source: str | None = None) -> dict:
+    """The revision-14 surface format as a GRADABLE_SURFACE: slots and selectors unchanged (tracker_entry and
+    register_parent included), work kinds inferred from the weights (from_slots), the mechanism ids (M1-M4) kept, and
+    the Season Pass deliverables, which that format leaves implicit."""
+    slots_doc = {"slots": [dict({"id": x["id"], "weight": x["weight"], "selector": x["selector"],
+                                 "title": x.get("title", "")},
+                                **({"gradable": x["gradable"]} if "gradable" in x else {}))
+                           for x in s.get("slots") or []], "source": f"{REV14_KIND} {source or ''}".strip()}
+    mech = {x["id"]: [x["mechanism"]] for x in s.get("slots") or [] if re.fullmatch(r"M\d+", str(x.get("mechanism")))}
+    out = from_slots(slots_doc, task="season_pass", revision="rev14", sql_sha256=s.get("sql_sha256"),
+                     deliverables=deliverables or json.loads(json.dumps(SEASON_PASS_DELIVERABLES)),
+                     classes=list(((s.get("truth") or {}).get("classes") or {"READY": 0, "WATCH": 0, "HOLD": 0})),
+                     mechanisms=mech)
+    out["provenance"].update(rule="from-revision-14: selectors as written; work kinds inferred from weights; the "
+                                  "Season Pass deliverables of season_pass_runs_rev14_fixed.py",
+                             revision14_truth=s.get("truth"), revision14_sampling_groups=s.get("sampling_groups"),
+                             revision14_known_gaps=s.get("known_gaps"))
+    return out

-def validate(surface: dict, manifest: dict | None = None, rule_ids=None) -> dict:
+def _split_top(rx: str) -> list:
+    """Split a regex on top-level `|` (never inside a class, group or escape)."""
+    parts, depth, start, i = [], 0, 0, 0
+    while i < len(rx):
+        ch = rx[i]
+        if ch == "\\":
+            i += 2
+            continue
+        if ch == "[":
+            i += 1
+            while i < len(rx) and rx[i] != "]":
+                i += 2 if rx[i] == "\\" else 1
+            i += 1
+            continue
+        if ch == "(":
+            depth += 1
+        elif ch == ")":
+            depth -= 1
+        elif ch == "|" and depth == 0:
+            parts.append(rx[start:i])
+            start = i + 1
+        i += 1
+    parts.append(rx[start:])
+    return parts
+
+
+def _rx_candidates(rx: str, cap: int = 64) -> list:
+    """A few literal strings a regex can match, from a tiny structural generator (enough for fixture keys and
+    names). Not a general regex sampler: unsupported atoms give no candidate, and the caller falls back to the
+    regex's literal chunks."""
+    parts = _split_top(rx)
+    if len(parts) > 1:
+        out = []
+        for p in parts:
+            out.extend(_rx_candidates(p, cap))
+            if len(out) >= cap:
+                break
+        return out[:cap]
+    toks, i = [], 0
+    while i < len(rx):
+        ch = rx[i]
+        if ch == "\\":
+            nxt = rx[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(rx) else ""
+            if nxt == "d":
+                atom = ["0"]
+            elif nxt == "w":
+                atom = ["a"]
+            elif nxt == "s":
+                atom = [" "]
+            elif nxt == "b":
+                atom = [""]
+            elif nxt in "DWSBAZz0123456789":
+                return []
+            else:
+                atom = [nxt]
+            i += 2
+        elif ch in "^$":
+            i += 1
+            continue
+        elif ch == ".":
+            atom = ["a"]
+            i += 1
+        elif ch == "[":
+            j = i + 1
+            while j < len(rx) and rx[j] != "]":
+                j += 2 if rx[j] == "\\" else 1
+            body = rx[i + 1:j]
+            if not body or body.startswith("^"):
+                return []
+            atom = [body[1] if body.startswith("\\") and len(body) > 1 else body[0]]
+            i = j + 1
+        elif ch == "(":
+            depth, j = 1, i + 1
+            while j < len(rx) and depth:
+                if rx[j] == "\\":
+                    j += 2
+                    continue
+                depth += 1 if rx[j] == "(" else (-1 if rx[j] == ")" else 0)
+                j += 1
+            inner = rx[i + 1:j - 1]
+            if inner.startswith("?:"):
+                inner = inner[2:]
+            elif inner.startswith("?P<"):
+                k = inner.find(">")
+                if k < 0:
+                    return []
+                inner = inner[k + 1:]
+            elif inner.startswith("?"):
+                return []
+            atom = _rx_candidates(inner, cap)
+            if not atom:
+                return []
+            i = j
+        elif ch in "*+?":
+            return []
+        else:
+            atom = [ch]
+            i += 1
+        lo = 1
+        if i < len(rx) and rx[i] == "{":
+            j = rx.find("}", i)
+            if j < 0:
+                return []
+            try:
+                lo = int(rx[i + 1:j].split(",")[0])
+            except ValueError:
+                return []
+            i = j + 1
+        elif i < len(rx) and rx[i] in "*+?":
+            ch2 = rx[i]
+            lo = 0 if ch2 in "*?" else 1
+            i += 1
+        toks.append((atom, lo))
+    combos = [""]
+    for atom, lo in toks:
+        combos = [(c + a * lo) for c in combos for a in atom[:4]][:cap]
+    return combos[:cap]
+
+
+def _unesc(m) -> str:
+    c = m.group(1)
+    if c in "bB":
+        return " "
+    if c == "d":
+        return "0"
+    if c == "w":
+        return "a"
+    if c == "s":
+        return " "
+    return c
+
+
+def _literal_chunks(rx: str) -> list:
+    """The literal runs of a regex (per top-level alternative), e.g. `\\bREADY\\b\\D{0,20}?\\b44\\b` -> READY, 44."""
+    out = []
+    for part in _split_top(str(rx)):
+        s = re.sub(r"\\(.)", _unesc, part)
+        s = re.sub(r"[\[\]{}()*+?^$|]", " ", s)
+        for m in re.finditer(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 ._@/-]*", s):
+            t = m.group(0).strip(" .")
+            if t:
+                out.append(t)
+    return out
+
+
+def sample_from_regex(rx: str):
+    """A string the regex matches, or None. Tries the structural generator, then the literal chunks alone, joined and
+    adjacent (the shape most slot patterns use, e.g. "SHIP 38")."""
+    rx = str(rx)
+    try:
+        re.compile(rx)
+    except re.error:
+        return None
+
+    def hit(cand):
+        try:
+            return bool(cand) and re.fullmatch(rx, cand) is not None
+        except re.error:
+            return False
+    cands = _rx_candidates(rx)
+    chunks = _literal_chunks(rx)
+    cands += chunks + [" ".join(chunks), "".join(chunks)]
+    for i in range(len(chunks) - 1):
+        cands += [chunks[i] + " " + chunks[i + 1], chunks[i] + chunks[i + 1]]
+    for c in cands:
+        if hit(c):
+            return c
+    return None
+
+
+# ------------------------------------------------------------------ surface.gradeable: a dry run through the real decoder
+
+def _table_for_key(tables: list, key) -> tuple:
+    """(name, spec) of the table deliverable whose key_regex matches this key, else (None, None)."""
+    for name, spec in tables:
+        try:
+            if re.fullmatch(spec.get("key_regex") or r".+", str(key)):
+                return name, spec
+        except re.error:
+            continue
+    return None, None
+
+
+def _key_for(spec: dict, hints=()) -> str | None:
+    krx = spec.get("key_regex") or r".+"
+    for h in hints:
+        if h is not None and re.fullmatch(krx, str(h)):
+            return str(h)
+    return sample_from_regex(krx)
+
+
+def _keys_for(spec: dict, n: int, hint=None) -> list:
+    """n distinct keys that each fullmatch the spec's key_regex, or fewer if it cannot be done. Numeric suffixes are
+    scanned (not just the next n integers), so a regex like `K-(?:01|03)` still yields two valid keys."""
+    krx = spec.get("key_regex") or r".+"
+    key = _key_for(spec, [hint] if hint else [])
+    out = []
+
+    def ok(k):
+        try:
+            return bool(re.fullmatch(krx, k)) and k not in out
+        except re.error:
+            return False
+    if key and ok(key):
+        out.append(key)
+    m = re.search(r"(\d+)(\D*)$", key or "")
+    if m:
+        start, width, tail = int(m.group(1)), len(m.group(1)), m.group(2)
+        for i in range(start, start + 400):
+            if len(out) >= n:
+                break
+            k = key[:m.start(1)] + f"{i:0{width}d}" + tail
+            if ok(k):
+                out.append(k)
+    if len(out) < n and key and not m:
+        return out
+    return out[:n]
+
+
+def _cell_value(comp: str | None, key: str) -> str:
+    return {"issue_key": str(key), "pr_number": "PR #1", "sha_prefix": "abcdef0", "yes_no": "yes",
+            "norm": "ok"}.get(comp or "norm", "ok")
+
+
+def _table_name(spec: dict) -> str:
+    """A file name the table deliverable's name_regex accepts (the CSV write must carry it)."""
+    return sample_from_regex(spec.get("name_regex") or "") or "deliverable"
+
+
+def _csv_call(name: str, columns: list, row: dict | list, result: dict | None = None, parent: str | None = None) -> tuple:
+    rows = row if isinstance(row, list) else [row]
+    buf = io.StringIO()
+    wr = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator="\n")
+    wr.writerow(columns)
+    for r in rows:
+        wr.writerow([r.get(c, "") for c in columns])
+    q = {"name": name, "mimeType": "text/csv", "content": buf.getvalue()}
+    if parent:
+        q["parentId"] = parent
+    return ("gdrive_create_file", q, result or {"id": "DRYRUNFILE", "ok": True})
+
+
+def _dry_run_call(calls: list):
+    import trajectory_ledger as tl
+    cs, rs = [], {}
+    for i, c in enumerate(calls):
+        cs.append({"event": i, "id": f"c{i}", "name": f"mcp__hg__{c[0]}", "input": c[1], "model": "dry-run",
+                   "parent": None, "context": "main"})
+        rs[f"c{i}"] = {"event": i, "error": False, "data": c[2] if len(c) > 2 else {"ok": True}}
+    return tl.Run("dry-run", Path("dry-run.json"), "derived-calls", None, cs, rs)
+
+
+def _selector_problem(slot: dict):
+    """Why the slot's selector is not one this tool can evaluate, or None. Shared by gradeable() and the report so
+    they never disagree, and called before any fixture is built so a malformed selector cannot crash the CLI (R5-2)."""
+    sel = slot.get("selector")
+    if not isinstance(sel, list) or not sel or not isinstance(sel[0], str) or sel[0] not in SELECTORS:
+        return "malformed selector"
+    if len(sel) - 1 < SELECTORS[sel[0]]:
+        return f"selector {sel[0]} needs {SELECTORS[sel[0]]} argument(s)"
+    if sel[0] == "delivery":
+        if not isinstance(sel[1], str):
+            return f"delivery check {sel[1]!r} is not a name"
+        need = DELIVERY_CHECKS.get(sel[1])
+        if need is None or len(sel) - 2 < need:
+            return f"unknown delivery check {sel[1:]!r}"
+    if sel[0] == "tracker_count":
+        n = sel[2]
+        if isinstance(n, bool) or not isinstance(n, int) or n < 0:
+            return f"tracker_count needs a non-negative integer count, not {n!r}"
+    if sel[0] == "tracker_entry" and (not isinstance(sel[3], list) or not sel[3]):
+        return "tracker_entry needs a non-empty list of value patterns"
+    if sel[0] in ("reason_matches", "text"):
+        if not isinstance(sel[2], str):
+            return f"regex argument {sel[2]!r} is not a string"
+        try:
+            re.compile(sel[2])
+        except (re.error, TypeError) as error:
+            return f"bad regex {sel[2]!r}: {error}"
+    return None
+
+
+def _heading_line(section: str) -> str:
+    """A tracker heading line the selector's heading regex matches: the sample alone when it already looks like a
+    heading, else `### <sample>`, and never a double markdown prefix (R5-5)."""
+    sample = sample_from_regex(section) or section
+    for line in (sample, f"### {sample}"):
+        try:
+            if re.search(section, line, re.I):
+                return line
+        except re.error:
+            break
+    return sample
+
+
+def _dry_run(surface: dict, slot: dict) -> dict:
+    """Build a synthetic truth side and write payload for one slot, decode and grade it. GRADABLE only when the slot
+    reads True on a fixture built for it *and* False on the same truth with no content delivered (a slot that passes
+    with nothing written is not gradable)."""
+    import surface_grader as sg
+    sid, sel = slot.get("id"), slot.get("selector")
+    tables = table_deliverables(surface)
+    tname, tspec = tables[0] if tables else (None, None)
+    xname, xspec = text_deliverable(surface)
+    classes = surface.get("classes") or ["READY"]
+    tf = surface.get("truth_fields") or {}
+    tclass = tf.get("class") or (tspec or {}).get("class_column") or "class"
+    tgroups = tf.get("groups") if isinstance(tf.get("groups"), str) else "groups"
+    cf = (tspec or {}).get("class_column") or tclass
+    res = {"slot": sid, "weight": slot.get("weight"), "selector": sel,
+           "status": "UNGRADABLE", "reason": None, "kind": None}
+    problem = _selector_problem(slot)                 # R7-3: validated before any fixture, declared or not
+    if problem:
+        return dict(res, reason=problem)
+    kind = sel[0]
+    res["kind"] = kind
+
+    def ungradable(reason):
+        return dict(res, status="UNGRADABLE", reason=reason)
+
+    def check(truth, calls, why=""):
+        try:
+            d = sg.decode(_dry_run_call(calls), surface)
+            got = sg.grade(surface, truth, d).get(sid)
+            empty = sg.grade(surface, truth, sg.decode(_dry_run_call([]), surface)).get(sid)
+        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - a decoder that raises on the class is the finding
+            return ungradable(f"decoder raised {type(error).__name__}: {error}")
+        if got is None:
+            return ungradable(f"UNGRADED with a fixture built for it{': ' + why if why else ''}")
+        if not got:
+            return ungradable(f"no synthetic fixture the slot reads (graded False){': ' + why if why else ''}")
+        if empty is None:
+            return ungradable("UNGRADED when nothing is delivered")
+        if empty:
+            return ungradable("grades True with nothing delivered (the fixture cannot fail)")
+        return dict(res, status="GRADABLE", reason=None)
+
+    if kind == "field":
+        key, field = str(sel[1]), str(sel[2])
+        tn, tsp = _table_for_key(tables, key)
+        if tsp is None:
+            return ungradable(f"field key {key!r} matches no table deliverable's key_regex")
+        if field not in (tsp.get("columns") or []):
+            return ungradable(f"column {field!r} is not a column of table {tn!r}")
+        val = key if field == tsp.get("key_column") else _cell_value((tsp.get("comparators") or {}).get(field), key)
+        row = {c: (key if c == tsp.get("key_column") else (val if c == field else "")) for c in tsp["columns"]}
+        truth = {key: {tclass: val if field == cf else classes[0], tgroups: [], field: val}}
+        return check(truth, [_csv_call(_table_name(tsp), list(tsp["columns"]), row)])
+
+    if kind in ("total", "printed_total"):
+        cls = str(sel[1])
+        if tspec is None or cf not in (tspec.get("columns") or []):
+            return ungradable("no table deliverable with a class column")
+        tn = tname
+        cols2 = _keys_for(tspec, 2)
+        if len(cols2) < 2:
+            return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize two keys for key_regex {tspec.get('key_regex')!r}")
+        key, keys = cols2
+        rows = [{c: (k if c == tspec.get("key_column") else (cls if c == cf else "")) for c in tspec["columns"]}
+                for k in (key, keys)]
+        truth = {k: {tclass: cls, tgroups: []} for k in (key, keys)}
+        if kind == "printed_total":
+            totals = {tspec["columns"][0]: cls, tspec["columns"][1]: "2"}
+            calls = [_csv_call(_table_name(tspec), list(tspec["columns"]), rows + [totals])]
+        else:
+            calls = [_csv_call(_table_name(tspec), list(tspec["columns"]), rows)]
+        return check(truth, calls, "two rows of one class")
+
+    if kind in ("group_count", "group_size"):
+        group = str(sel[1])
+        if any(name == group for name, _ in tables):
+            return ungradable(f"group {group!r} names the table deliverable {group!r}: no tracker text declares a "
+                              f"group of that name, so the count has no written source")
+        if xspec is None:
+            return ungradable(f"no text deliverable declares a tracker: group {group!r} has no written source")
+        krx = xspec.get("key_regex") or tspec.get("key_regex") if (xspec or tspec) else r".+"
+        gk = _keys_for({"key_regex": krx}, 2)
+        if len(gk) < 2:
+            return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize two keys for key_regex {krx!r}")
+        k1, k2 = gk
+        text = f"- {group} (2):\n- {k1}\n- {k2}"     # multi-line: the decoder must keep every member (G1 R8)
+        truth = {k1: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: [group]}, k2: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: [group]}}
+        return check(truth, [(xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue", {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "description": text})])
+
+    if kind in ("group_key", "groups_of", "sole_reason"):
+        key = str(sel[1])
+        if xspec is None:
+            return ungradable("no text deliverable declares a tracker: the group has no written source")
+        names = [g for g, _ in (xspec.get("groups") or [])]
+        group = str(sel[2]) if kind != "groups_of" else (names[0] if names else "group")
+        text = f"- {group} (1): {key}"
+        truth = {key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: [group]}}
+        return check(truth, [(xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue", {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "description": text})])
+
+    if kind == "reason_matches":
+        key, rx = str(sel[1]), str(sel[2])
+        tn, tsp = _table_for_key(tables, key)
+        if tsp is None:
+            return ungradable(f"key {key!r} matches no table deliverable's key_regex")
+        col = tsp.get("reason_column")
+        if not col or col not in (tsp.get("columns") or []):
+            return ungradable(f"table {tn!r} declares no reason_column")
+        val = sample_from_regex(rx)
+        if val is None:
+            return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a value for regex {rx!r}")
+        row = {c: (key if c == tsp.get("key_column") else (val if c == col else "")) for c in tsp["columns"]}
+        return check({key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: [], col: val}}, [_csv_call(_table_name(tsp), list(tsp["columns"]), row)])
+
+    if kind in ("tracker_lists", "tracker_count", "tracker_entry"):
+        if xspec is None:
+            return ungradable("no text deliverable declares a tracker")
+        krx = xspec.get("key_regex") or r".+"
+        key = _key_for({"key_regex": krx}, [sel[2] if len(sel) > 2 else None])
+        if key is None:
+            return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a key for key_regex {krx!r}")
+        if kind == "tracker_entry":
+            section, value, pats = str(sel[1]), str(sel[2]), list(sel[3])
+            vals = [sample_from_regex(p) for p in pats]
+            if any(v is None for v in vals):
+                return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a value for entry patterns {pats}")
+            text = f"{_heading_line(section)}\n- {value}: {' '.join(vals)}"
+        else:
+            section = str(sel[1])
+            n = int(sel[2]) if kind == "tracker_count" else 1
+            if kind == "tracker_count" and n < 0:
+                return ungradable(f"tracker_count {n} is negative")
+            keys = _keys_for({"key_regex": krx}, n, key)
+            if len(keys) < n:
+                return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize {n} distinct keys for key_regex {krx!r}")
+            text = _heading_line(section) + "".join(f"\n- {k}" for k in keys)
+        return check({key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: []}},
+                     [(xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue", {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "description": text})])
+
+    if kind == "text":
+        target, rx = str(sel[1]), str(sel[2])
+        sample = sample_from_regex(rx)
+        if sample is None:
+            return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a string for regex {rx!r}")
+        if any(name == target for name, _ in tables):
+            tn, tsp = next((n, s) for n, s in tables if n == target)
+            key = _key_for(tsp)
+            if key is None:
+                return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a key for key_regex {tsp.get('key_regex')!r}")
+            row = {c: (key if c == tsp.get("key_column") else "") for c in tsp["columns"]}
+            row[next((c for c in tsp["columns"] if c != tsp.get("key_column")), tsp["columns"][0])] = sample
+            return check({key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: []}}, [_csv_call(_table_name(tsp), list(tsp["columns"]), row)])
+        d_spec = (surface.get("deliverables") or {}).get(target)
+        tool = (d_spec or {}).get("tool")
+        if target == "tracker" or (d_spec or {}).get("kind") == "text":
+            if xspec is None:
+                return ungradable(f"text target {target!r} is a tracker with no text deliverable")
+            return check({}, [(xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue",
+                               {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "description": sample})])
+        if d_spec is None and target not in ("slide", "email", "slack", "tickets", "summary", "mail"):
+            return ungradable(f"text target {target!r} is not a deliverable")
+        # the same implicit tool names decode() uses for the legacy json_text targets
+        tool = tool or {"slide": "gslides_batch_update", "email": "gmail_reply_to_message",
+                        "slack": "slack_send_message"}.get(target, target)
+        return check({}, [(tool, {"text": sample, "body": sample, "message": sample})])
+
+    if kind == "link":
+        target = str(sel[1])
+        if tspec is None:
+            return ungradable("link needs a table deliverable to carry an identity")
+        key = _key_for(tspec)
+        if key is None:
+            return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a key for key_regex {tspec.get('key_regex')!r}")
+        row = {c: (key if c == tspec.get("key_column") else "DRYRUNFILE") for c in tspec["columns"]}
+        calls = [_csv_call(_table_name(tspec), list(tspec["columns"]), row, result={"id": "DRYRUNFILE", "ok": True}),
+                 ("gdrive_share_file", {"fileId": "DRYRUNFILE", "role": "reader"})]
+        dst = next((sp for n, sp in tables if n == target and n != tname), None)
+        if dst is not None:
+            # R5-7: the identity must reach the *selected* destination table, so write it into that table too
+            dk = _keys_for(dst, 1)
+            if not dk:
+                return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a key for destination table {target!r}")
+            drow = {c: (dk[0] if c == dst.get("key_column") else "DRYRUNFILE") for c in dst["columns"]}
+            calls.append(_csv_call(_table_name(dst), list(dst["columns"]), drow, result={"id": "DRYRUNDST"}))
+        elif target not in [n for n, _ in tables]:
+            d_spec = (surface.get("deliverables") or {}).get(target)
+            tool = (d_spec or {}).get("tool")
+            if target == "tracker" or (d_spec or {}).get("kind") == "text":
+                if xspec is None:
+                    return ungradable(f"link target {target!r} is a tracker with no text deliverable")
+                tool = xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue"
+                calls.append((tool, {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "description": "DRYRUNFILE shared"}))
+            else:
+                tool = tool or {"slide": "gslides_batch_update", "email": "gmail_reply_to_message",
+                                "slack": "slack_send_message"}.get(target, target)
+                calls.append((tool, {"text": "DRYRUNFILE shared", "body": "DRYRUNFILE shared"}))
+        return check({key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: []}}, calls)
+
+    if kind == "delivery":
+        name = str(sel[1])
+        if name == "register_is_csv":
+            if tspec is None:
+                return ungradable("register_is_csv needs a table deliverable")
+            key = _key_for(tspec)
+            if key is None:
+                return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a key for key_regex {tspec.get('key_regex')!r}")
+            return check({key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: []}},
+                         [_csv_call(_table_name(tspec), list(tspec["columns"]), {tspec.get("key_column"): key})])
+        if name == "register_keys_exact":
+            if tspec is None:
+                return ungradable("register_keys_exact needs a table deliverable")
+            tn = str(sel[2]) if len(sel) > 2 else tname
+            tsp = next((s for n, s in tables if n == tn), None)
+            if tsp is None:
+                return ungradable(f"register_keys_exact names {tn!r}, which is not a table deliverable")
+            expected = [str(k) for k in (slot.get("expected_keys") or surface.get("truth_keys") or [])]
+            if not expected and slot.get("role") == "volume":
+                k = _key_for(tsp)                       # a volume row's cohort is the whole register
+                expected = [k] if k else []
+            if not expected:
+                return ungradable("register_keys_exact has no explicit expected key set (truth_keys or slot "
+                                  "expected_keys): it would grade against the whole truth")
+            krx = tsp.get("key_regex") or r".+"
+            bad = [k for k in expected if not re.fullmatch(krx, k)]
+            if bad:
+                return ungradable(f"expected key(s) {bad[:3]} do not match table {tn!r}'s key_regex")
+            key = expected[0]
+            row = [{c: (k if c == tsp.get("key_column") else "") for c in tsp["columns"]} for k in expected]
+            return check({k: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: []} for k in expected},
+                         [_csv_call(_table_name(tsp), list(tsp["columns"]), row)])
+        if name in ("share_reader", "register_parent"):
+            if tspec is None:
+                return ungradable(f"{name} needs a table deliverable identity")
+            key = _key_for(tspec)
+            if key is None:
+                return ungradable(f"cannot synthesize a key for key_regex {tspec.get('key_regex')!r}")
+            parent = str(sel[2]) if name == "register_parent" else None
+            calls = [_csv_call(_table_name(tspec), list(tspec["columns"]), {tspec.get("key_column"): key},
+                               result={"id": "DRYRUNFILE", "ok": True}, parent=parent),
+                     ("gdrive_share_file", {"fileId": "DRYRUNFILE", "role": "reader"})]
+            return check({key: {tclass: classes[0], tgroups: []}}, calls)
+        if name == "slide_updated":
+            return check({}, [("gslides_batch_update", {"presentationId": "P1", "requests": [{"x": "y"}]})])
+        if name in ("tracker_state", "tracker_preserves"):
+            arg = str(sel[2])
+            if name == "tracker_preserves":
+                if xspec is None:
+                    return ungradable("tracker_preserves needs a text deliverable")
+                return check({}, [(xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue",
+                                   {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "description": f"kept {arg}"})])
+            if arg in ("comment", "description", "state"):
+                return check({}, [("trello_add_comment_to_card", {"cardId": "C1", "text": f"{arg} noted"})])
+            if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{24}", arg):
+                return check({}, [("trello_add_comment_to_card", {"cardId": arg, "text": "noted"})])
+            if xspec is None:
+                return ungradable(f"tracker_state {arg!r} needs a text deliverable (its state is read from tracker "
+                                  f"updates)")
+            # R7-2: the runtime uses `re.match(arg, state, re.I)`, so the fixture state must match the regex, not be
+            # the regex text (an anchored `^Done$` would otherwise be rejected by the dry run)
+            state = arg
+            try:
+                if re.match(arg, arg, re.I) is None:
+                    state = sample_from_regex(arg) or arg
+            except re.error:
+                return ungradable(f"bad tracker_state regex {arg!r}")
+            return check({}, [(xspec.get("tool") or "linear_update_issue", {"id": xspec.get("issue"), "state": state})])
+        if name == "trello_card":
+            return check({}, [("trello_add_comment_to_card", {"cardId": str(sel[2]), "text": str(sel[3])})])
+        if name == "email_reply":
+            return check({}, [("gmail_reply_to_message", {"message_id": str(sel[2]), "reply_all": False,
+                                                          "body": "received"})])
+        if name == "slack_thread":
+            return check({}, [("slack_send_message", {"thread_ts": str(sel[2]), "text": "noted"})])
+        if name == "slack_channel_post":
+            return check({}, [("slack_send_message", {"channel_id": str(sel[2]), "text": str(sel[3])})])
+        if name == "slack_after_email":
+            return check({}, [("gmail_reply_to_message", {"message_id": "m1", "reply_all": False, "body": "x"}),
+                              ("slack_send_message", {"channel_id": "C1", "text": "noted"})])
+        return ungradable(f"no dry-run fixture for delivery check {name!r}")
+
+    return ungradable(f"no dry-run fixture for selector kind {kind!r}")
+
+
+def _gradeable_structure(surface: dict) -> list:
+    """Structural limits a synthetic fixture cannot repair (kept from the pre-dry-run check): a Slack thread must be
+    addressed by a timestamp; a tracker_state must not be a Trello card id (that is a different check); an exact-key
+    check needs an explicit expected key set unless it is a volume row (whose cohort is the whole register)."""
+    out, tf = [], surface.get("truth_fields") or {}
+    tname, tspec = table_deliverable(surface)
+    group_slots = [s for s in surface.get("slots") or []
+                   if isinstance(s.get("selector"), list) and s.get("selector")
+                   and s["selector"][0] in ("group_count", "group_size", "group_key", "groups_of",
+                                            "tracker_lists", "tracker_count")]
+    if group_slots and not tf.get("groups"):
+        out.append({"slot": None, "kind": "group",
+                    "reason": "group/tracker slots need truth_fields.groups to build their truth side"})
+    if not isinstance(tf.get("groups"), (str, type(None))):
+        out.append({"slot": None, "kind": "group",
+                    "reason": f"truth_fields.groups is {tf.get('groups')!r}: it must name a field, not hold a value"})
+    gname = tf.get("groups")
+    if isinstance(gname, str):
+        for tn, tsp in table_deliverables(surface):
+            if gname in (tsp.get("columns") or []):
+                out.append({"slot": None, "kind": "group",
+                            "reason": f"truth_fields.groups names {gname!r}, a column of table {tn!r}: a written "
+                                      f"column is one scalar per row, never a group list"})
+                break
+    for s in surface.get("slots") or []:
+        sel, sid = s.get("selector"), s.get("id")
+        if not isinstance(sel, list) or not sel or not isinstance(sel[0], str) or sel[0] not in SELECTORS:
+            continue
+        if len(sel) - 1 < SELECTORS[sel[0]]:
+            continue
+        if sel[0] == "delivery":
+            if not isinstance(sel[1], str):
+                out.append({"slot": sid, "kind": "delivery", "reason": f"delivery check {sel[1]!r} is not a name"})
+                continue
+            need = DELIVERY_CHECKS.get(sel[1])
+            if need is None or len(sel) - 2 < need:
+                continue
+            if sel[1] == "slack_thread" and not re.fullmatch(r"\d{10}\.\d{6}", str(sel[2])):
+                out.append({"slot": sid, "kind": "delivery",
+                            "reason": f"slack_thread value {sel[2]!r} is not a Slack timestamp"})
+            if sel[1] == "tracker_state":
+                v = str(sel[2])
+                if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{24}", v) or re.fullmatch(r"T-?\d+", v):
+                    out.append({"slot": sid, "kind": "delivery",
+                                "reason": f"tracker_state value {v!r} looks like a Trello id, not a state"})
+            if sel[1] == "register_keys_exact":
+                if not tspec:
+                    out.append({"slot": sid, "kind": "delivery",
+                                "reason": "register_keys_exact needs a table deliverable"})
+                elif not (s.get("expected_keys") or surface.get("truth_keys")) and s.get("role") != "volume":
+                    out.append({"slot": sid, "kind": "delivery",
+                                "reason": "register_keys_exact has no explicit expected key set (truth_keys or slot "
+                                          "expected_keys): it would grade against the whole truth"})
+    return out
+
+
+def gradeable(surface: dict) -> list:
+    """surface.gradeable: a dry run of every slot through the grader's real decoder.
+
+    For each slot: build a synthetic truth side and write payload for that selector class, run surface_grader.decode()
+    and grade(), and require the slot to read True; the same truth with nothing delivered must read False (a slot
+    that passes with no content is not a certification). Returns one record per slot that cannot be read, with the
+    reason; validate() turns them into FAIL findings, so a surface with an ungradable slot never passes."""
+    out = [{"slot": r["slot"], "weight": next((s.get("weight") for s in surface.get("slots") or []
+                                                if s.get("id") == r["slot"]), None), "kind": r.get("kind"),
+            "status": "UNGRADABLE", "reason": r["reason"]} for r in _gradeable_structure(surface)]
+    seen = {r["slot"] for r in out}
+    for s in surface.get("slots") or []:
+        sel = s.get("selector")
+        if s.get("id") in seen:
+            continue
+        if not isinstance(sel, list) or not sel or not isinstance(sel[0], str) or sel[0] not in SELECTORS:
+            continue                                  # structural validation reports malformed selectors
+        if len(sel) - 1 < SELECTORS[sel[0]]:
+            continue
+        if sel[0] == "delivery" and (DELIVERY_CHECKS.get(sel[1]) is None or len(sel) - 2 < DELIVERY_CHECKS[sel[1]]):
+            continue
+        r = _dry_run(surface, s)
+        if r["status"] == "UNGRADABLE":
+            out.append(r)
+    return out
+
+
+def gradeable_report(surface: dict) -> dict:
+    """Every slot with its dry-run status (for the `report` command and the task-1 evidence file). The structural
+    limits gradeable() enforces are part of the verdict, so the report and validate() never disagree."""
+    rows, structurally = [], {}
+    for r in _gradeable_structure(surface):
+        structurally.setdefault(r.get("slot"), r["reason"])
+    for s in surface.get("slots") or []:
+        r = _dry_run(surface, s)
+        if s.get("id") in structurally:
+            r = dict(r, status="UNGRADABLE", reason=structurally[s["id"]])
+        rows.append(r)
+    for sid, reason in structurally.items():                 # a surface-level finding with no slot of its own
+        if sid is None:
+            rows.append({"slot": None, "weight": None, "selector": None, "status": "UNGRADABLE", "reason": reason,
+                         "kind": "group"})
+    counts = collections.Counter(r["status"] for r in rows)
+    return {"surface_task": surface.get("task"), "surface_revision": surface.get("revision"),
+            "slots": rows, "counts": dict(counts),
+            "verdict": "PASS" if not counts.get("UNGRADABLE") else "FAIL"}
+
+
+def truth_group_problems(truth: dict, surface: dict) -> list:
+    """The group field named by truth_fields.groups must be a list on every truth row (a scalar is not membership)
+    and must be a field name, never a value."""
+    tf = surface.get("truth_fields") or {}
+    gf = tf.get("groups")
+    if gf is not None and not isinstance(gf, str):
+        return [f"truth_fields.groups is {gf!r}: it must name the field that holds each row's group list"]
+    gf = gf or "groups"
+    out = []
+    for k, v in (truth or {}).items():
+        if not isinstance(v, dict) or gf not in v:
+            continue
+        if not isinstance(v[gf], list):
+            out.append(f"truth[{k}].{gf} is {type(v[gf]).__name__} {v[gf]!r}: a group list is required")
+    return out
+
+

+def _range_only(line: str, krx: str) -> bool:
+    """Every key the line names is an endpoint of a key range: prose about the cohort, not a key list (defect 6).
+
+    G1-9/R2-1: compared as multisets, so an explicitly listed member that happens to duplicate a range endpoint
+    ("Review (1): K-01; cohort K-01..K-20") keeps the line as prose only when no occurrence is left over. The range
+    itself is found by position between two key matches, so a caller's named-group regex is never interpolated twice."""
+    keys = collections.Counter(m.group(0) for m in re.finditer(krx, line))
+    if not keys:
+        return False
+    ends = collections.Counter()
+    for a, b in _range_pairs(line, krx):
+        ends.update((a, b))
+    return keys == ends
+
+
+_RANGE_JOIN = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:\.\.|–|—|\bthrough\b|\bthru\b|\bto\b)\s*$", re.I)
+
+
+def _range_pairs(line: str, krx: str) -> list:
+    """[(a, b)] for every adjacent pair of key matches joined only by a range connector."""
+    ms = list(re.finditer(krx, line))
+    return [(a.group(0), b.group(0)) for a, b in zip(ms, ms[1:])
+            if _RANGE_JOIN.match(line[a.end():b.start()])]
+
+
+def _expand_range(a: str, b: str):
+    """The keys of one cohort range (same prefix, same-width zero-padded suffix), or None if it is not a range."""
+    ma, mb = re.fullmatch(r"(.*?)(\d+)", a), re.fullmatch(r"(.*?)(\d+)", b)
+    if not ma or not mb or ma.group(1) != mb.group(1) or len(ma.group(2)) != len(mb.group(2)):
+        return None
+    start, end, width = int(ma.group(2)), int(mb.group(2)), len(ma.group(2))
+    if end < start or end - start > 1000:
+        return None
+    return [f"{ma.group(1)}{n:0{width}d}" for n in range(start, end + 1)]
+
+
+def _expand_keys(line: str, krx: str) -> list:
+    """Every key the line names, with each range expanded to its members (R2-1); duplicates count once."""
+    ms = list(re.finditer(krx, line))
+    out, i = [], 0
+    while i < len(ms):
+        a = ms[i].group(0)
+        if i + 1 < len(ms) and _RANGE_JOIN.match(line[ms[i].end():ms[i + 1].start()]):
+            b = ms[i + 1].group(0)
+            out.extend(_expand_range(a, b) or [a, b])
+            i += 2
+        else:
+            out.append(a)
+            i += 1
+    return list(dict.fromkeys(out))
+
+
+_DELIM_RE = re.compile(r"\|?\s*:?-{2,}:?\s*(\|\s*:?-{2,}:?\s*)*\|?")
+
+
+def _table_row(s: str) -> bool:
+    """A Markdown table row or header: an UNESCAPED pipe separator, and neither a heading nor a bullet list item (a
+    bullet that carries a pipe is a list item, not a table row; an escaped pipe inside a cell does not unmake a row)."""
+    t = s.lstrip()
+    if re.match(r"#{1,6}(?: |$)", t) or re.match(r"[-*+](?:[ \t]+|$)", t):
+        return False
+    return "|" in s.replace("\\|", "")
+

+def _headingish(line: str) -> bool:
+    """A section heading is a markdown # line, a fully bold/underlined line, or a short colon-terminated line with no
+    list marker and no ". "; a list item (even one ending in ':') is never a heading (R2-3)."""
+    s = line.strip()
+    if re.match(r"(?:[-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+", s):
+        return False
+    return bool(re.match(r"#{1,6}\s", s) or re.fullmatch(r"(\*\*|__)[^*_]+(\*\*|__):?", s)
+                or (len(s) <= 80 and s.endswith(":") and ". " not in s))
+
+
+def _section_start(lines: list, heading_regex: str, krx: str):
+    """The heading line of the section headed by heading_regex, or None: the first matching heading-like line, else
+    the first matching line that names keys after the match, else the first match (defect 1)."""
+    hits = [i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if re.search(heading_regex, line, re.I)]
+    if not hits:
+        return None
+    start = next((i for i in hits if _headingish(lines[i])), None)
+    if start is None:
+        start = next((i for i in hits if re.search(krx, lines[i][re.search(heading_regex, lines[i], re.I).end():])),
+                     hits[0])
+    return start
+
+
+def _section_body(lines: list, start: int, krx: str) -> list:
+    """Every line after the heading up to the next heading-like line; intro prose and table headers don't end it
+    (defect 2)."""
+    body = []
+    for nxt in lines[start + 1:]:
+        if _headingish(nxt):
+            break
+        body.append(nxt)
+    return body
+
+
+def _section(text: str, heading_regex: str, krx: str):
+    """(heading tail, body lines) of the section headed by heading_regex, or None."""
+    lines = text.split("\n")
+    start = _section_start(lines, heading_regex, krx)
+    if start is None:
+        return None
+    return lines[start][re.search(heading_regex, lines[start], re.I).end():], _section_body(lines, start, krx)
+
+
+_KEY_SEP = r"(?:\s*(?:,|;|/|&|\+|·|\band\b|\bor\b)\s*)+"    # what may separate two keys of a bare list (R2-2)
+_RX_TAIL = re.compile(r"[\s.,;:)\]}\*_`]*")            # what may follow the last key of a list (R2-2/R3-3)
+
+
+def _list_shape(tail: str, krx: str) -> bool:
+    """Whether `tail` is a bare key list: keys separated only by separators and closed by punctuation. The key regex
+    is used once, through spans, so a named-group regex never collides (R3-3)."""
+    ms = list(re.finditer(krx, tail))
+    if not ms or tail[:ms[0].start()].strip(" \t*_`"):
+        return False
+    gap = re.compile(_KEY_SEP, re.I)
+    pos = ms[0].end()
+    for m in ms[1:]:
+        if not gap.fullmatch(tail[pos:m.start()]):
+            return False
+        pos = m.end()
+    return bool(_RX_TAIL.fullmatch(tail[pos:]))
+
+
+def _md_clean(s: str) -> str:
+    """Strip inline markdown emphasis/backticks per key, not only at the ends (R2-2)."""
+    return re.sub(r"\*\*|__|`", " ", s)
+
+
+def _bare_key_list(body: str, krx: str) -> bool:
+    """Whether the line body is a grammatical key list: an optional label ending in ':', then keys joined only by
+    separators or and/or (Oxford commas included), then nothing but closing punctuation (G1-5/R2-2). The old
+    character-count allowance accepted short prose ("K-01 depends on K-02"); a list is a shape, not a length."""
+    tail = re.sub(r"^[\s*_`]+", "", body)
+    label = re.match(r"^[^:]{0,120}:\s*", tail)          # "Owner calls:" / "**Review not approved (2):**"
+    if label:
+        tail = tail[label.end():]
+    tail = _md_clean(re.sub(r"^[\s*_`]+", "", tail)).strip()
+    return _list_shape(tail, krx)
+
+
+def _keys_in(text: str, krx: str) -> list:
+    """Every key of the text: the whole regex match, never a capture group (R3-2: a partial or nested named capture
+    would otherwise yield "K" or a tuple)."""
+    return [m.group(0) for m in re.finditer(krx, text)]
+
+
+def _row_keys(line: str, krx: str) -> list:
+    """Keys a list line names: a table row's first cell; every key of a bare key list, bulleted or not (defect 5); a
+    prose bullet's first key. Prose and header lines name none. G1-5/R2-2: a bare key list is recognised by its
+    grammar (keys separated only by separators or and/or, Oxford commas and per-key markdown included)."""
+    s = line.strip()
+    keys = _keys_in(s, krx)
+    if not keys:
+        return []
+    if s.startswith("|"):
+        return _keys_in(s.strip("|").split("|")[0], krx)[:1]
+    bullet = re.match(r"([-*•]|\d+[.)])\s+", s)
+    body = s[bullet.end():] if bullet else s
+    tail = re.sub(r"^[\s*_`]+", "", body)
+    label = re.match(r"^[^:]{0,120}:\s*", tail)
+    if label:
+        if _keys_in(label.group(0), krx):
+            return keys[:1] if bullet else []        # a key inside the label: prose, not a label
+        tail = tail[label.end():]
+    tail = _md_clean(re.sub(r"^[\s*_`]+", "", tail)).strip()
+    if _list_shape(tail, krx):
+        return _keys_in(tail, krx)
+    return keys[:1] if bullet else []
+
+
+def tracker_section(text: str, heading_regex: str, key_regex: str, count: bool = False):
+    """Keys in the section headed by heading_regex, or None. Every key in the section counts as listed; an exact count
+    uses the list-like lines when the section has any, so a prose aside doesn't inflate it."""
+    sec = _section(text, heading_regex, key_regex)
+    if sec is None:
+        return None
+    lines = [sec[0]] + sec[1]
+    every = [k for line in lines for k in _keys_in(line, key_regex)]
+    listed = [k for line in lines for k in _row_keys(line, key_regex)]
+    return listed if count else every
+
+
+def tracker_section_lines(text: str, heading_regex: str, key_regex: str):
+    sec = _section(text, heading_regex, key_regex)
+    if sec is None:
+        return None
+    return [line for line in [sec[0]] + sec[1] if re.search(key_regex, line)]
+
+
+def _entry_match(line: str, pattern: str, known_hex: set) -> bool:
+    """A tracker_entry value: a regex, or a SHA value (7-40 hex characters) that also matches an unambiguous prefix of
+    7 or more characters (defect 8; 09_Rubric_Writing gate 5). Ambiguous: another known SHA shares the prefix.
+
+    G1-6: an abbreviated expected value is resolved against the known hashes WITHOUT the pattern itself (the
+    selector's own text is not evidence); two incomparable extensions are ambiguous, so False; the resolved target
+    and the pattern are never rivals of a written token."""
+    raw = str(pattern).strip()
+    low = raw.lower()
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{7,40}", low):
+        return bool(re.search(raw, line, re.I))       # a regex keeps its case (\D, \A, ...)
+    pat = low
+    known = {str(h).strip().lower() for h in known_hex
+             if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{7,40}", str(h).strip(), re.I)}
+    known.discard(pat)
+    ext = sorted({h for h in known if len(h) > len(pat) and h.startswith(pat)})
+    # a chain H[:8] / H[:16] / H is one target (the longest); incomparable extensions are ambiguous
+    targets = [h for h in ext if not any(h != o and o.startswith(h) for o in ext)]
+    if len(targets) > 1:
+        return False
+    target = targets[0] if targets else pat
+    for tok in RX_HEX.findall(line.lower()):
+        if not target.startswith(tok):   # the written value must be the target or a prefix of it
+            continue
+        rivals = {h for h in known if not (target.startswith(h) or h.startswith(target))
+                  and (h.startswith(tok) or tok.startswith(h))}
+        if not rivals:                   # one known hash shares the written prefix: unambiguous
+            return True
+    return False
+
+
+def _patch(q: dict):
+    p = q.get("patch")
+    if isinstance(p, str):
+        try:
+            p = json.loads(p)
+        except ValueError:
+            pass
+    return p
+
+
+def tracker_updates(w: dict, tool: str, issue: str | None) -> list:
+    """Successful updates of the tracking issue in run order, matched exactly (whitespace only, G1-7); every update of
+    the tool when the deliverable names no issue. An update of a sibling issue ("T-10" for "T-1", "ZOM-8010" for
+    "ZOM-801") never supplies the tracker's state or content, and there is no fallback to unrelated updates."""
+    ups = sorted(w.get(tool, []), key=lambda c: c["event"])
+    if not issue:
+        return ups
+    want = str(issue).strip()
+    return [c for c in ups if str(c["input"].get("id") or c["input"].get("issueId") or "").strip() == want]
+
+
+def tracker_final_state(ups: list) -> str:
+    """The tracking issue's state after every update in order: the last update that sets one (defect 4)."""
+    for c in reversed(ups):
+        state = c["input"].get("state") or c["input"].get("stateId")
+        if state:
+            return str(state)
+    return ""
+
+
+def _mutations(ups: list) -> list:
+    """[(event, kind, text)] for every content mutation of the description, in order: kind is "replace", "append",
+    "prepend", or "unknown" (a mutation this decoder cannot read; text is ""). G1-2: a full description and a
+    `replace /description` string replace the content; append/prepend modify it; a patch op or path that is not a
+    description mutation ("/labels") changes no content, so it adds no uncertainty."""
+    out = []
+    for c in ups:
+        q, p = c["input"], _patch(c["input"])
+        event = c["event"]
+        if "description" in q:                         # an explicit empty full description clears the content
+            v = q["description"]
+            out.append((event, "replace", v) if isinstance(v, str) else (event, "unknown", ""))
+        if p is None:
+            continue
+        if isinstance(p, str):
+            out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+        elif isinstance(p, dict):
+            kind0 = str(p.get("op") or "").lower()
+            path0 = str(p.get("path") or "").lstrip("/")
+            if kind0 == "test" or (kind0 == "move" and "from" in p
+                                   and str(p.get("from") or "").lstrip("/") == path0):
+                continue                               # a test op and a move to the same path are no-ops (R2-4)
+            path = str(p.get("path") or "")
+            if "description" in p and (not path or path.lstrip("/") == "description"):
+                v = p["description"]
+                out.append((event, "replace", v) if isinstance(v, str) else (event, "unknown", ""))
+            elif p:
+                out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+        elif isinstance(p, list):
+            for op in p:
+                if not isinstance(op, dict):
+                    out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                    continue
+                kind = str(op.get("op") or "").lower()
+                text = op.get("text")
+                value = op.get("value")
+                path_given = "path" in op
+                path = str(op.get("path") or "")
+                sub = path.lstrip("/")
+                if kind == "test":
+                    continue                           # JSON Patch test changes no content (R2-4)
+                if kind == "move" and "from" in op:
+                    src = str(op.get("from") or "").lstrip("/")
+                    if src == sub:
+                        continue                       # a move to its own path is a no-op (R2-4)
+                    if src == "description":
+                        out.append((event, "replace", ""))
+                    elif src.startswith("description/"):
+                        out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                if not path_given:                      # the documented pathless append/prepend form
+                    if kind in ("append", "prepend") and isinstance(text, str):
+                        out.append((event, kind, text))
+                    elif kind in ("replace", "add") and isinstance(value, str):
+                        out.append((event, "replace", value))
+                    else:
+                        out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                    continue
+                if path == "":                          # JSON Pointer root, not the pathless form
+                    if kind in ("replace", "add") and isinstance(value, dict) and "description" in value:
+                        v = value["description"]
+                        out.append((event, "replace", v) if isinstance(v, str) else (event, "unknown", ""))
+                    elif kind in ("replace", "add", "remove", "move", "copy"):
+                        out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                    continue
+                if sub == "description":
+                    if kind in ("append", "prepend") and isinstance(text, str):
+                        out.append((event, kind, text))
+                    elif kind in ("replace", "add") and isinstance(value, str):
+                        out.append((event, "replace", value))
+                    elif kind == "remove":
+                        out.append((event, "replace", ""))     # R7-4: a deletion is a known empty replacement
+                    elif kind in ("replace", "add"):
+                        out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                    else:
+                        out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                elif sub.startswith("description/"):
+                    out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+                else:
+                    continue                       # another field (e.g. /labels): no content, no uncertainty
         else:
-            pending = None
-    return groups
-

-                break
-            keys.extend(found)
-        return keys
-    return None
+            out.append((event, "unknown", ""))
+    return out
+
+
+def tracker_text(ups: list) -> tuple:
+    """(effective description, unreadable events): the content replayed in order (G1-2/G1-4). A full description or
+    a `replace /description` string replaces the text, append/prepend modify it, and an unreadable mutation is
+    recorded so its slots become UNGRADED, never false; a later full replacement clears it because it re-establishes
+    the content."""
+    text, unreadable = "", []
+    for event, kind, value in _mutations(ups):
+        if kind == "replace":
+            text, unreadable = value, []
+        elif kind == "append":
+            text = f"{text}\n{value}" if text else value
+        elif kind == "prepend":
+            text = f"{value}\n{text}" if text else value
+        else:
+            unreadable.append(event)
+    return text, unreadable
+
+
+def tracker_preserved(ups: list, original: str):
+    """True | False | None: whether the original text survives every update in order (defect 4). A replacement that
+    drops it is a known violation and stays False even if a later write restores it; append/prepend keep it; an
+    unreadable mutation makes the history unknown (None), never false, and a later replacement cannot prove what
+    happened in between."""
+    state = True
+    for _, kind, value in _mutations(ups):
+        if kind == "replace":
+            if original not in value:
+                state = False                 # a known violation is absorbing
+            elif state is True:
+                state = True
+        elif kind == "unknown":
+            if state is not False:
+                state = None
+    return state

+def _strings(v, out: list) -> list:
+    if isinstance(v, str):
+        out.append(v)
+    elif isinstance(v, dict):
+        for x in v.values():
+            _strings(x, out)
+    elif isinstance(v, list):
+        for x in v:
+            _strings(x, out)
+    return out
+

+def _register_parent(calls: list, results: dict, file_id: str):
+    """The final folder of the shared register: the creation parentId, then any later successful move."""
+    parent = None
+    for c in calls:
+        n, q, r = tl.short(c["name"]), c["input"] or {}, results.get(c["id"])
+        if tl.failed(r):
+            continue
+        data = r.get("data") if isinstance(r, dict) else None
+        made = (data.get("id") or data.get("spreadsheetId")) if isinstance(data, dict) else None
+        if n in ("gdrive_create_file", "gsheets_create_spreadsheet") and made == file_id:
+            parent = q.get("parentId") or q.get("folderId") or parent
+        elif n == "gdrive_move_file" and q.get("fileId") == file_id:
+            parent = q.get("newParentId") or parent
+    return parent
+
+
+def _content_strings(calls: list) -> str:
+    """The readable content of json_text deliverable calls: every content string at every nesting level, never an
+    identifier parameter (R2-6: a text slot must not match an objectId/presentationId)."""
+    vals = []
+
+    def content(v):
+        if isinstance(v, dict):
+            for k, x in v.items():
+                kl = str(k).lower()
+                # R3-2: identifier parameters at any nesting level are never readable content: every *id/*ids key
+                # (channel_id, cardId, issueId, fileId, objectId, presentationId, ...) and the raw locator params.
+                if not (kl.endswith(("id", "ids")) or kl in ("channel", "ts", "thread_ts", "threadts")):
+                    content(x)
+        elif isinstance(v, list):
+            for x in v:
+                content(x)
+        elif isinstance(v, str):
+            vals.append(v)
+    for c in calls:
+        content(c["input"])
+    return "\n".join(vals)
